Active gradient function:
```
def active_gradient(corr, h, alpha, top=64):
   M=corr.max()
   k=min(len(corr), top)
   idx=np.argpartition(corr, -k)[-k:]
   diff=corr[idx]-M
   # diff <=0
   expv=np.exp(alpha*diff)
   s=expv.sum()
   if s <=0: # fallback
   w=expv/s
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for p,wt in zip(idx,w):
       s_shift = p - (n-1)
       # first part: derivative of h[i+s](1-h_i) wrt h_j
       if s_shift >=0:
           g[s_shift:n] += wt*(1.0 - h[:n-s_shift])
           g[:n-s_shift] -= wt*h[s_shift:n]
       else:
           t=-s_shift
           g[:n-t] += wt*(1.0 - h[t:n])
           g[t:n] -= wt*h[:n-t]
   return g
```
Check dimensions for s positive:
First: j=s..n-1, h[j-s] = h[0..n-s-1], add 1-h[0:n-s]. g[s:n] length n-s. Good.
Second: j=0..n-s-1, h[j+s]=h[s:n], subtract. g[0:n-s]. Good.
For s negative: s=-t. Derivative first: j-s = j+t valid j=0..n-t-1, add 1-h[t:n] to g[0:n-t]. Good. second: j+s=j-t valid j=t..n-1, subtract h[j-t]=h[0:n-t] from g[t:n]. Good.
This gradient is for corr, not area. For S = M + (1/α)log sum exp(α(corr-M)), derivative = weighted gradient of corr (if weights normalized over active). Yes (soft max derivative). If using top tail, approximate.

SLSQP call:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h0,n,alpha,top,maxiter,deadline,ftol):
   def fun(x): return soft_obj(x,n,alpha,top)
   def jac(x): return soft_obj_jac(x,n,alpha,top)
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':lambda x:x.sum()-n/2.0,'jac':lambda x:np.ones(n)}] # SLSQP may accept jac?
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   res=minimize(fun,h0,..., jac=jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
   return res.x
```

_STATE = {}
def _slsqp_obj(x):
    a=_STATE['alpha']; top=_STATE['top']; n=_STATE['n']
    corr = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    M=corr.max()
    k=min(corr.size, top)
    idx=np.argpartition(corr,-k)[-k:]
    base=a*(corr[idx]-M)
    e=np.exp(base)
    s=e.sum()
    if s ==0: return M
    return M + np.log(s)/a
def _slsqp_jac(x):
    ... return grad
def _eq_fun(x): return x.sum() - _STATE['target']
def _eq_jac(x): return _STATE['ones']
```

def pg_optimize(h0,n,deadline,alpha0=20,alpha1=2000,max_iter=20000):
    target=n/2; h=project(...)
    v=np.zeros(n); beta=alpha0
    step=0.5
    S_prev = soft_obj_value(h,alpha)
    best...
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time > deadline: break
       beta schedule
       corr, M, weights = ...
       grad = active_grad(...)
       # maybe use smoothed objective S
       # Barzilai-Borwein step if have previous step
       if have lastx,lastg:
          d=h-lasth; gg=grad-lastg; rho=sum(d*d)/sum(gg*gg); step=sum(d*gg)/rho if sign
          step=clip(step,0.01,5)
       # line search
       accept=False
       for ls in range(10):
           h1=project(h-step*grad)
           S1=soft_obj_value(h1,beta)  # recomputes corr
           if S1 < S_prev - 1e-12*max(1, step*gnorm) or ls==? accept
           else step*=0.7
       if accept: update, S_prev=S1; gn update; else reduce step
```

Pseudo:
```
def subgradient_polish(h,n,deadline, start_alpha=50, end_alpha=5000, top=32, step0=0.05):
    target=n/2
    h=project(h)
    best...
    step=step0
    beta=start_alpha
    for phase in range(phases):
       beta = ...
       active_n = top maybe 10,30,80
       v=0
       for it until phase_deadline:

          weights top active softmax

State global:
```
_G = {}
def _obj(x):
    n=_G['n']; alpha=_G['alpha']; top=_G['top']
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    M=c.max()
    k=top if top < c.size else c.size
    # We can restrict to top k for both value and gradient to be consistent
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    val=c[idx]
    base=alpha*(val-M)
    e=np.exp(base)
    return M + np.log(e.sum())/alpha
```
But if M from all c and tail ignored, value overestimates? Actually log over top only; M max all. Fine.

Let's implement `indices_weights_from_corr(corr, alpha, top)`:

- If alpha <=0: weights uniform over top.

Gradient from idx/w:
```
def grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w):
   n=len(h); center=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
   for p,wt in zip(idx,w):
      if wt < 1e-12: continue
      s=p-center
      if s>=0:
         if s>0: # slice lengths
             g[s:n] += wt*(1.0-h[:-s])
             g[:n-s] -= wt*h[s:n]
         else:
             g += wt*(1.0-h) - wt*h  # 1-2h
      else:
         t=-s
         g[:n-t] += wt*(1.0-h[t:])
         g[t:n] -= wt*h[:n-t]
   return g
```
For s=0, our general s>=0 first works: g[0:n] += 1-h[:n]; g[:n] -= h. Good. But when s=0, `h[:-s]` becomes h[:0]? Need handle separately because n-s=n, `h[:-0]` empty. So code:
```
if s > 0:

Now `delta_pair` exactly:
```
def pair_delta(h, corr, a,b,t):
   delta = np.zeros_like(corr)
   add_point_gradient_linear(delta, a, +t, h)
   add_point_gradient_linear(delta, b, -t, h)
   delta[n-1] -= 2*t*t
   if a != b:
      delta[n-1 + (a-b)] += t*t
      delta[n-1 + (b-a)] += t*t
   return delta
```
But the linear part computed as derivative wrt h at old values times δ. We can implement add_linear_point(delta,q,δ,h): For each lag s? That would be O(n²) if per point per lag. For q and δ:

  For s such that 0≤q+s<n: s=q-i ranges? i=q+s => s=i-q, i=q..n-1. delta[s] -= δ*h[i].

Let's verify first term: For fixed q, first derivative term is δ_q(1-h_{q-s}) for s such that j=q-s in 0..n-1. Let j=q-s => s=q-j, p=s+n-1=q-j+n-1. For j=0 -> p=n-1+q; j=n-1 -> p=q. So p increasing as j decreasing. If we set vector p=q..q+n-1 (inclusive length n), corresponding j = n-1 + q - p. For p=q, j=n-1; for p=q+n-1, j=0. Second term: derivative -δ_q h_{q+s} for i=q+s in q..n-1. s=i-q, p=n-1+i-q. For i=q -> p=n-1; i=n-1 -> p=2n-2-q. Values h[q:]. So `delta[n-1:2n-1-q] -= amt*h[q:]`. Then pair cross: For two points with changes +t at a, -t at b. The exact product correction sum -∑ δ_{i+s}δ_i. Let's verify with this point-add formula already includes linear only. Add cross:
- For each pair of changes (u,v) values du,dv, the quadratic correction for shift s=u-v? The term -δ_u δ_v if u = i+s, v=i => s=u-v. Also if u=i, v=i+s => s=v-u. For each ordered pair, correction = -du*dv for s? Let's derive from -∑_i δ_{i+s}δ_i. For a given nonzero du at u, dv at v. Contributions:
  if i=v, i+s=u => s=u-v: term -du*dv.
  if i=u, i+s=v => s=v-u: term -du*dv.
If both u=v (not), includes squares twice.
Here du=+t, dv=-t => du*dv=-t². Correction +t² at s=a-b and s=b-a. For s=0, squares: -du² -dv² = -2t². So above correct.

def local_search_gradient_pairs(h0,n,deadline,max_moves=50000,recompute_interval=64,top=32,alpha=100,noise=0.0,rng=None):
   target=n/2; h=project_box_mean(h0,target); corr=corr_full(h); cur=float(corr.max())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
   om=1-h; # om_rev view? If we set om_rev = om[::-1] once, when h updated and om updated, view points to om; okay if om not reallocated. Do not do om=1-h later; update in place.
   om_rev = om[::-1]
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
   # grad:
   idx,w = soft_indices_from_corr(corr, alpha, top)
   g = grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w)
   hi = np.where(g > np.percentile(g,75))[0]
   lo = np.where(g < np.percentile(g,25))[0]
   tmp = np.zeros_like(corr)
   moves=0
   nonimp=0
   for moves in range(max_moves):
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      if moves % recompute_interval ==0:
         # recompute corr? We maintain corr exactly; but after projection maybe drift. Use cur=corr.max.
         cur=corr.max()
         if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
             best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
         idx,w=soft_indices_from_corr(corr, alpha, top)
         g=grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w)
         if len(g)>0:

            hi=np.where(g > ng + 0.1*max(1e-6, np.std(g)))[0]
            lo=np.where(g < ng - 0.1*...)[0]

             hi=np.where(g>0)[0]; lo=np.where(g<0)[0]
         if len(hi)==0 or len(lo)==0:
             # small random perturb? break
             break
      if len(hi)==0 or len(lo)==0: break
      a=hi[rng.randint(len(hi))]
      found=False
      # try some b
      num_b = 8 + (moves % 16)? maybe 16
      for _ in range(16):

         if maxt <= 1e-9: continue

         for r in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):

             if t<=1e-12: continue

             tmp[n-1:2*n-1-b] -= (-t) * h[b:]  # = + t*h[b:]

def slsqp_top_variant(h0,n,alpha,top,maxiter,deadline):
   _STATE['n']=n; ... target; ones
   h=project_box_mean(h0,n/2)
   cons={'type':'eq','fun':_eqfun,'jac':_eqjac}
   bounds=((0.0,1.0),)*n
   t=time.time()
   # maybe first estimate if maxiter unknown
   res=minimize(_obj,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=[cons],jac=_jac,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'disp':False})
   h=project_box_mean(res.x if res.x is not None else h,n/2)
   return h
```

def projected_smooth_top(h0,n,deadline, alphads, tops, step0, max_iters):
   target=n/2; h=project; best...
   total_deadline=deadline
   for alpha,topy in zip:
      phase_dead = min(deadline, time.time()+some fraction)
      step=step0; S_prev, g, corr, M = smooth_pack(...); x_old=h; g_old=g
      for it in range(max_iters) until phase_dead:
         if time>phase_dead: break
         corr_max=corr.max(); c=...
         update best
         # BB step
         if g_old is not None:
            d=h-x_old; gd=g-g_old; denom=np.dot(gd,gd); if denom>1e-12: step=(d@gd)/denom
            step=clip(step,1e-5,0.5)
         # line search 5
         accepted=False
         for ls in range(8):
             h1=project_box_mean(h - step*g, target)
             S1, _, _, _ = smooth_pack(h1, alpha, top) # expensive
             if S1 <= S_prev + 1e-12:
                 accepted=True; break
             step*=0.7
         if accepted:
             h=h1; S_prev=S1; x_old...; g_old=g; # need recompute g for new? next loop
             # But after accept, we didn't recompute g at h1. Next loop computes.
         else:
             # perturb? maybe just break or small random
             step=max(step*0.3,1e-6)
             # perhaps if many fail, add noise
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  start=time.time(); deadline=start+budget_s-3
  # load parent
  ...
  best_h,best_c,best_n = project/eval
  # keep a copy of initial for restarts
  # Phase 1: SLSQP at parent n (small)
  if time<deadline-20:
     h = slsqp_optimize_params(best_h, n0, [(1,256,est),(10,128),(50,64),(200,32)], phase_budget= min(60,0.15*budget), ...)
     evaluate update
  # Phase 2: pair local search at parent n
  if time<deadline-10: h,c,n = local_search_pairs(best_h,n0, deadline_pair, ...)
     update
  # Phase 3: multi-resolution SLSQP/polish
  candidate_ns = []
  for ns in [int(round(1.5*n0)), int(round(2*n0)), int(round(3*n0)) ]:
      if ns even, not candidate_n, 300<=ns<=2000: add
  Sort by maybe n? start with 2*n0? Use current best as source each time.
  For each ns with remaining:
      hres=resample(best_h,best_n, ns)
      eval update
      # SLSQP at ns if remaining > 50
      hsls=run_slsqp_sequence(hres, ns, phases based on remaining)
      update
      # pair search at ns using remaining
      hloc,cloc=local_search_pairs(hsls/ns, ns, deadline_ns)
      update
      # Optionally if improved, continue to higher ns
  # Phase 4: final pair search at best_n with all remaining
  hloc,cloc=local_search_pairs(best_h,best_n, deadline-2, max_moves large)
  update
  # final project, exact eval
  return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

def slsqp_refine(h0,n,phases,phase_budget,deadline):
   h=project_box_mean(h0,n/2)
   if phase_budget <= 10 or time.time()>=deadline: return h
   # estimate using first phase? We'll do loop; for each phase, before big maxiter estimate remaining.
   for alpha,K in phases:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       _STATE.update(...)
       # Estimate with 3 iters if remaining > 30, else small
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       maxiter=50
       # maybe trial cost if n>300
       t_est=time.time()
       # Limit trial to min(3, budget)
       res=minimize(_smooth_obj,h,... maxiter=3)
       dt=(time.time()-t_est)/3.0
       if dt<=0: dt=1
       # target use at most 80% rem for this phase? Since multiple phases, distribute equal? We'll spend max(5, min(20, rem*0.4?)). Simpler allocate each phase budget=phase_budget/len(phases).
       phase_each=phase_budget/len(phases)
       maxiter=int(min(200, max(10, 0.7*phase_each/dt)))
       res=minimize(...)
       h=project(res.x)
   return h
```

Now `resample_h` and `project_box_mean` ensure area. For n large, project bisection O(80n). Fine.

Let's think about exact target `sum(h)==n/2 exactly`. Our project bisection uses 80 iterations; sum within ~1e-14 maybe. Then adjust one element. But adjusting one can push outside [0,1] if diff > room; but diff small. If n=0 no.
Use `diff = target - h.sum()`; if >0, choose idx with max `1-h`, add diff; but if diff > 1-h[idx]? Not if diff small. For safety, distribute diff across all room: `h += diff * room / room.sum()` if diff not tiny; then clip; then adjust. But this could break [0,1] if diff large? It won't if room sum >0. Let's implement robust final projection:
```
def project_box_mean(h,target):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-13:
       # if target exact maybe adjust rounding? return
    # bisection
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; sr=room.sum()
          if sr>1e-14:
             h += diff*room/sr
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(h==1? no))
       else:
          shrink=-diff; s=h.sum(); h -= shrink*h/s if s>...
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff != 0:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1-h)
          add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
          # distribute remainder
          if diff>0:
             room=1-h; sr=room.sum(); if sr>0: h += diff*room/sr
       else:
          idx=np.argmax(h)
          sub=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
          if diff<0: h += diff*h/h.sum()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
But repeated distribution may not exact due to float. We can force exact by adjusting one coordinate after final with enough tolerance. If target is e.g., 125.0, h.sum() may be 125.00000000000001; diff = -1.4e-14; adjust last. The harness likely uses allclose. Good. We can simply after clipping do:
```
diff = target - h.sum()
if diff != 0:

Let's verify pair_delta formula with a small example to ensure no off-by-one.
Take n=3, corr from h. Suppose only change at q with δ. Our add_point formula:
First: tmp[q:q+n] += δ*(1-h_rev) with h_rev indices [h2,h1,h0]. For q=1:
 tmp[1:4] (p=1,2,3) += δ*[1-h2,1-h1,1-h0]
Second: tmp[2:2*3-1-1=4] (p=2,3) -= δ*h[1:]=[h1,h2]
Total delta p:
 p0:0
 p1: δ*(1-h2)
 p2: δ*(1-h1) -δ*h1 = δ*(1-2h1)
 p3: δ*(1-h0) -δ*h2
Expected derivative for point q=1 over shifts s=p-(n-1)=p-2:
 s=-2 (p0): q-s=1-(-2)=3 invalid; q+s=-1 invalid ->0.
 s=-1 (p1): first q-s=2 valid: δ*(1-h2); second q+s=0 invalid -> 1-h2. Good.
 s=0 (p2): δ*(1-h1)-δ*h1. Good.
 s=1 (p3): first q-s=0 valid δ*(1-h0); second q+s=2 valid -δ*h2. Good.
 s=2 (p4): none. Works.
For pair +t at a, -t at b, cross:
Example n=3, a=0 (+t), b=1(-t). Cross +t² at p + (0-1)=-1 -> p=1, and p=3. Zero p=2 -2t².
Expected quadratic terms for s=-1: i? δ_{i-1}δ_i with + at0,- at1. If s=-1, i+s? Need formula -δ_{i+s}δ_i. For s=-1: i-1? If i=1: δ_0 δ_1 = -t² -> +t². p=1. Good. For s=1: i=0: δ_1 δ_0 = -t² -> +t² p=3. s=0: -δ0²-δ1²=-2t² p=2. Good.

Thus formula correct.

Now pair search t choices: If we use t=1.0*maxt, h values may saturate; if h[src] or h[dst] becomes 0/1, future updates maxt=0 for that pair. Fine. If source underflows negative due to t=h[src] exactly? float; t = min(h[src],1-h[dst]) * r; if r=1, add/sub subtract can leave 0 with small -1e-16? We clip? After updating, we can if h[src]<0 set 0, adjust dst? But sum may drift. Simpler after accept:

```

```
h[src] = max(0.0, h[src]-t)
h[dst] = min(1.0, h[dst]+t)
# if change differs due to clip, adjust other to preserve sum:
actual_src = old_src - h[src]; actual_dst = h[dst] - old_dst; if actual_src != actual_dst: adjust the one with room? Hard.
```

om[dst] = 1 - h[dst]

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_STATE = {}
_CACHE = {'x': None, 'c': None}

def _eval_corr(x):
    return np.correlate(np.asarray(x), 1.0 - np.asarray(x), mode='full')

def c5_exact(h, n):
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))

def project_box_mean(h, target):
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
   h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
   s=h.sum()
   if abs(s-target)<1e-14:
      # still exact adjust?
   else:
      lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
      # if target > sum at mid? We'll just bisection always
      for _ in range(80):
         mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
         sh=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
         if sh > target: lo=mid
         else: hi=mid
      h=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if diff != 0.0:
      if diff > 0:
         idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
         avail=1.0-h[idx]
         add=min(diff,avail)
         h[idx]+=add
         diff-=add
         if abs(diff)>1e-15:
            room=1.0-h; sr=room.sum()
            if sr>1e-15:
               h += diff*room/sr
               h=np.clip(h,0,1)
               # final fix
               d=target-h.sum()
               if d>0:
                  idx2=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx2]=min(1,h[idx2]+d)
               elif d<0:
                  idx2=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx2]=max(0,h[idx2]+d)
      else:
         idx=int(np.argmax(h))
         sub=min(-diff,h[idx])
         h[idx]-=sub
         diff+=sub
         if abs(diff)>1e-15:
            s=h.sum()
            if s>1e-15:
               h += diff*h/s
               h=np.clip(h,0,1)
               d=target-h.sum()
               if d>0: idx2...; elif d<0...
   # final ensure exact-ish
   d=target-h.sum()
   if d>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+d)
   elif d<0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+d)
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This is messy; can simplify: after bisection, h sum within 1e-12. Do one final adjust with max room. If bisection failed because target not reached? target feasible. Use:
```
d=target-h.sum()
if d>1e-12:
   idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]+=min(d,1-h[idx])
elif d<-1e-12:
   idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]+=max(d,-h[idx])
h=np.clip(...)
```

def resample_h(h_old,n_old,n_new):
   if n_new<=0: return np.array([])
   if n_new==n_old:
      return project_box_mean(h_old,n_new/2)
   x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*(2.0/n_old)
   x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)

`smooth_top_value` and `grad`:
```
def smooth_top_value(c, alpha, K):
   sz=c.size; k=min(K,sz)
   if k==sz:
      vals=c
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, sz-k)[-k:]
      vals=c[idx]
   m=vals.max()
   if alpha <= 1e-8:
      return float(m)
   base=alpha*(vals-m)
   # base <=0, max 0
   e=np.exp(base)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
      return float(m)
   return float(m + np.log(s)* (1.0/alpha))

def soft_weights(c, alpha, K):
   sz=c.size; k=min(K,sz)
   if k==sz:
      idx=np.arange(sz)
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, sz-k)[-k:]
   vals=c[idx]
   m=vals.max()
   if alpha <=1e-8:
      w=np.full(k,1.0/k)
      return idx,w
   base=alpha*(vals-m)
   # stable not needed since shifted by m
   e=np.exp(base)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
      # if underflow, put weight on max ones
      w=np.zeros(k); e_arg=np.flatnonzero(vals >= m - 1e-12); w[e_arg]=1/e_arg.size
   else:
      w=e/s
   return idx,w
```
If alpha large and exp underflows for all except max, s>=1 (max exp 0? exp(0)=1) so fine. If alpha infinite? no.

`grad_from_idx_weights`:
```
def grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w):
   n=len(h); center=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
   # For speed, maybe convert idx,w to arrays; skip small
   for p, wt in zip(idx, w):
      if wt <= 1e-14: continue
      s=p-center
      if s > 0:
         # first: g[s:n] += wt*(1-h[:n-s])
         seg=1.0 - h[:n-s]
         g[s:n] += wt*seg
         g[:n-s] -= wt*h[s:n]
      elif s < 0:
         t=-s
         seg=1.0 - h[t:n]
         g[:n-t] += wt*seg
         g[t:n] -= wt*h[:n-t]
      else:
         g += wt*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
   return g
```
Could optimize by prealloc? fine.

`_get_corr_cached(x)`:
```
def _get_corr_cached(x):
   last=_CACHE['x']
   if last is not None and len(last)==x.size:
       # use np.shares_memory? if same object? SLSQP may pass new array each call; we need value equality.
       # np.array_equal may be costly but okay.
       if np.array_equal(last, x):
          return _CACHE['c']
   c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
   _CACHE['x']=x.copy()
   _CACHE['c']=c
   return c
```
But if x values same but one nan? no.

SLSQP functions:
```
def _slsqp_obj(x):
   c=_get_corr_cached(x)
   return smooth_top_value(c, _STATE['alpha'], _STATE['K'])
def _slsqp_jac(x):
   c=_get_corr_cached(x)
   idx,w=soft_weights(c, _STATE['alpha'], _STATE['K'])
   return grad_from_idx_weights(x, idx, w)
def _eq_fun(x):
   return float(x.sum() - _STATE['target'])
def _eq_jac(x):
   return _STATE['ones']
```

`slsqp_phase`:
```
def slsqp_phase(h, n, alpha, K, phase_budget):
    target=n/2.0
    _STATE['n']=n; _STATE['target']=target; _STATE['ones']=np.ones(n)
    _STATE['alpha']=float(alpha); _STATE['K']=int(K)
    h=project_box_mean(h,target)
    bounds=((0.0,1.0),)*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_fun,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    opts={'disp':False}
    # estimate per iter using 2 iterations, but if budget small skip
    maxiter=30
    if phase_budget > 10:
       t_est=time.time()
       try:
          rés? no
          res2=minimize(_slsqp_obj, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_slsqp_jac, options={'maxiter':2,'ftol':1e-14,'disp':False})
          h2=project_box_mean(res2.x if res2.x is not None else h,target)
          elapsed=time.time()-t_est
       except Exception:
          elapsed=2.0; h2=h
       per=max(1e-6, elapsed/2.0)
       maxiter=int(max(5, min(120, 0.8*phase_budget/per)))
       h=h2
    opts['maxiter']=maxiter; opts['ftol']=1e-14
    try:
       res=minimize(_slsqp_obj,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,jac=_slsqp_jac,options=opts)
       if res.x is not None:
          h=project_box_mean(res.x,target)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return h
```
But this trial for each phase adds overhead. For multiple phases, 2 iters each okay. Note `_STATE['alpha']` used in trial then same for full. The trial may change h; good.
However if phase_budget large but SLSQP per-iter cost underestimated due to first 2 cheap, maxiter could overrun. Use safety 0.5. Also cap maxiter 80 for n>600, 150 for n<=600. Let's use:
```
cap=160 if n<=300 else 80 if n<=800 else 40
maxiter=int(max(5, min(cap, 0.6*phase_budget/per)))
```
This may leave time.

But if trial itself memory? okay.
Need set maxiter in options after trial; but full call after trial uses updated `_STATE` same.

`slsqp_refine(h,n,phases,total_budget,deadline)`:
```
def slsqp_refine(h,n,phases,total_budget,deadline):
   h=project_box_mean(h,n/2.0)
   phase_budget=total_budget/len(phases)
   for i,(alpha,K) in enumerate(phases):
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      rem=deadline-time.time()
      # ensure at least 3s remain for rest? phase_budget=min(total_budget/len, 0.8*rem)
      pb=min(phase_budget, max(3.0, 0.9*rem/ (len(phases)-i)))
      if pb <=5: continue
      h=slsqp_phase(h,n,alpha,K,pb)
      # Maybe if exact c worsened a lot, keep? We'll return h; best outside.
   return h
```

Need make top-level phases:
```
def make_slsqp_phases(n):
   K0 = min(2*n-1, max(64, n//2)) # n=250 ->128, 500->250, 1000->500 (too high maybe cap 320)
   K0 = min(K0, 320)
   Ks = [K0, max(32,K0//2), max(16,K0//4), max(16,K0//8)]
   As = [5.0, 50.0, 200.0, 1000.0]
   return list(zip(As,Ks))
```

`compute_grad_and_hi_lo(h,corr,alpha,K)`:
```
def gradient_high_low(h, corr, alpha=50.0, K=32):
   idx,w=soft_weights(corr,alpha,K)
   g=grad_from_idx_weights(h,idx,w)
   med=np.median(g)
   sd=float(np.std(g))
   if sd < 1e-14:
      return g, np.arange(len(h)), np.arange(len(h))
   thr_hi = med + 0.25*sd
   thr_lo = med - 0.25*sd
   hi=np.where(g >= thr_hi)[0]
   lo=np.where(g <= thr_lo)[0]
   if len(hi) < 10:
      hi=np.argsort(g)[-max(10,len(h)//10):]
   if len(lo) < 10:
      lo=np.argsort(g)[:max(10,len(h)//10)]
   return g,hi,lo
```
If g all positive (due to sum constraint?), hi high, lo lower but still positive. Move from high to low still decreases weighted gradient? Direction -g. Good.

`local_search_gradient_pairs` code in detail:
```
def local_search_gradient_pairs(h0,n,deadline,max_attempts=200000,recompute_interval=48,K=32,alpha=50.0,seed=0):
   target=n/2.0
   h=project_box_mean(h0,target)
   corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   cur=float(corr.max())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
   om=1.0-h
   om_rev=om[::-1]
   tmp=np.zeros(corr.size, dtype=float)
   buf=np.zeros(corr.size, dtype=float)
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   g,hi,lo=gradient_high_low(h,corr,alpha,K)
   stall=0; restarts=0; max_restarts=12; accepts=0; attempt=0
   tvals=(1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1) # maybe choose random
   while attempt < max_attempts and time.time() < deadline:
       if attempt % recompute_interval == 0:
           cur=float(corr.max())
           if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
               best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
           if accepts > 0 and accepts % 512 == 0:
               corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(corr.max())
           if stall > 600 and restarts < max_restarts and (deadline - time.time()) > 15:
               # perturb
               noise = 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
               # enforce zero mean roughly by subtract noise mean? project handles
               h = project_box_mean(h + noise, target)
               om=1.0-h; om_rev=om[::-1]
               corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(corr.max())
               if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
                  best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
               restarts += 1; stall=0
           g,hi,lo=gradient_high_low(h,corr,alpha,K)
           if len(hi)==0 or len(lo)==0:
              hi=np.arange(n); lo=np.arange(n)
       if len(hi)==0 or len(lo)==0:
          break
       src=hi[rng.randint(len(hi))]
       improved=False
       # try up to 8 destinations
       for _ in range(8):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          dst=lo[rng.randint(len(lo))]
          if src == dst: continue
          avail = min(h[src], 1.0 - h[dst])
          if avail <= 1e-10: continue
          # choose one t from tvals weighted random? Use all? Let's test one random t per pair to reduce.
          # Try a few: first avail (saturate), then half.
          for ratio in (1.0, 0.5):
              t=ratio*avail
              if t <= 1e-12: continue
              # compute delta
              tmp.fill(0.0)
              tmp[dst:dst+n] += t * om_rev
              tmp[n-1:2*n-1-dst] -= t * h[dst:]
              tmp[src:src+n] -= t * om_rev
              tmp[n-1:2*n-1-src] += t * h[src:]
              zzz=t*t
              tmp[n-1] -= 2.0*zzz
              if src != dst:
                  tmp[n-1 + (dst - src)] += zzz
                  tmp[n-1 + (src - dst)] += zzz
              np.add(corr, tmp, out=buf)
              nm=float(buf.max())
              if nm < cur - 1e-12:
                  # accept
                  h[src] -= t
                  h[dst] += t
                  # clip tiny
                  if h[src] < 0.0: h[src] = 0.0
                  if h[dst] > 1.0: h[dst] = 1.0
                  om[src] = 1.0 - h[src]
                  om[dst] = 1.0 - h[dst]
                  np.add(corr, tmp, out=corr)
                  cur=nm
                  best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
                  accepts += 1
                  improved=True
                  break
          if improved:
              break
       if improved:
          stall = 0
       else:
          stall += 8
       attempt += 1
   # final recompute to ensure exact
   corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   cur=float(corr.max())
   if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
       best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
   # project best
   best_h=project_box_mean(best_h,target)
   c=np.correlate(best_h,1-best_h,'full')
   best_cur=float(c.max())
   return best_h,best_cur
```

def smooth_value_and_grad(h, alpha, K):
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   S=smooth_top_value(c,alpha,K)
   idx,w=soft_weights(c,alpha,K)
   g=grad_from_idx_weights(h,idx,w)
   return S,g,c
```

def _get_anti_curves(u):
   last=_ANTI_CACHE['u']
   if last is not None and len(last)==u.size and np.array_equal(last,u):
      return _ANTI_CACHE['h'], _ANTI_CACHE['c']
   h=_anti_h(u)
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   _ANTI_CACHE['u']=u.copy(); _ANTI_CACHE['h']=h; _ANTI_CACHE['c']=c
   return h,c

def _anti_obj(u): h,c=_get_anti_curves(u); return smooth_top_value(c,_ASE['alpha'],_ASE['K'])
def _anti_jac(u):
   h,c=_get_anti_curves(u); alpha=...; idx,w=soft_weights(...); gh=grad_from_idx_weights(h,idx,w)
   p=_ASE['p']; n=_ASE['n']; gu=np.empty(p)
   # j indices
   for i in range(p): gu[i]=gh[i] - gh[n-1-i]
   return gu
```

Let's implement cache anti carefully. `_anti_curves` uses `_ASTATE` to build h. If `u` length differs from p due to L-BFGS-B, maybe pad? It should. In `_anti_obj`, if `len(u) != p`, take first p or clip? Could return a large penalty. Simpler assume.
```
def _anti_curves(u):
    p=_ASTATE['p']; n=_ASTATE['n']
    if u.size != p:
       u2=np.zeros(p); m=min(p,u.size); u2[:m]=u[:m]; u=u2
    last=_ACACHE['u']
    if last is not None and last.size==u.size and np.array_equal(last,u):
       return _ACACHE['h'], _ACACHE['c']
    h=np.empty(n)
    h[:p]=u
    if n%2:
       h[p]=0.5
       h[p+1:]=1.0-u[::-1]
    else:
       h[p:]=1.0-u[::-1]
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    _ACACHE['u']=u.copy(); _ACACHE['h']=h; _ACACHE['c']=c
    return h,c
```

`anti_phase`:
```
def anti_phase(u,n,alpha,K,phase_budget):
   p=n//2 if n%2==0 else (n-1)//2
   if p <=0: return u
   _ASTATE['n']=n; _ASTATE['p']=p; _ASTATE['alpha']=float(alpha); _ASTATE['K']=int(K); _ASTATE['mirror']=np.array([n-1-i for i in range(p)],dtype=int)
   u=np.clip(np.asarray(u,dtype=float),0,1)
   bounds=((0.0,1.0),)*p
   opts={'disp':False}
   maxiter=100
   if phase_budget > 5:
       t_est=time.time()
       try:
          res2=minimize(_anti_obj,u,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=_anti_jac,options={'maxiter':3,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'disp':False})
          if res2.x is not None: u2=np.clip(res2.x,0,1)
          else: u2=u
          elapsed=time.time()-t_est
       except Exception:
          u2=u; elapsed=1.0
       per=max(1e-4, elapsed/3.0)
       cap=500 if p<=250 else (300 if p<=600 else 150)
       maxiter=int(max(10, min(cap, 0.7*phase_budget/per)))
       u=u2
   opts['maxiter']=maxiter; opts['ftol']=1e-12; opts['gtol']=1e-8; opts['maxls']=20
   try:
       res=minimize(_anti_obj,u,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=_anti_jac,options=opts)
       if res.x is not None: u=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
   except Exception:
       pass
   return u
```

def anti_refine(h,n,phases,total_budget,deadline):
   h=anti_palindromic_h(h,n)
   p=n//2 if n%2==0 else (n-1)//2
   if p<=0: return h
   u=h[:p].copy()
   for idx,(alpha,K) in enumerate(phases):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       pb=min(total_budget/len(phases), 0.9*(rem/(len(phases)-idx)))
       pb=max(5.0, min(pb, rem-2.0))
       if pb <= 5: break
       u=anti_phase(u,n,alpha,K,pb)
       h=anti_h_from_u(u,n)
       # maybe if next time? continue
   return h
```

def make_anti_phases(n):
    scale = max(1.0, float(n)/250.0)
    K0 = min(2*n-1, max(64, n//2))
    K0 = min(K0, 512)
    Ks = (K0, max(64,K0//2), max(32,K0//4), max(16,K0//8))
    As = (2.0*scale, 20.0*scale, 100.0*scale, 500.0*scale)
    return list(zip(As,Ks))
```

def local_search_gradient_pairs(h0,n,deadline,K=32,alpha=50.0,recompute_interval=40,max_restarts=12,seed=0):

def gradient_high_low(h,corr,alpha,K):
   idx,w=soft_weights(corr,alpha,K)
   g=grad_from_idx_weights(h,idx,w)
   if g.size==0: return g, np.array([],int), np.array([],int)
   q=max(16, min(256, h.size//10))
   if len(g) > q:
      order_hi=np.argsort(g)[-q:]
      order_lo=np.argsort(g)[:q]
   else:
      order_hi=np.where(g==g.max())[0]
      order_lo=np.where(g==g.min())[0]
   # Extra random? q maybe includes many. Use unique.
   return g, order_hi, order_lo
```

Now exact `c5_value` function:
```
def c5_value(h,n):
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    B=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+B-4.0
    # load
    try:
       h_in=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       n_in=250

    n_in=len(h_in)
    if n_in <=2: ...
    h=project_box_mean(h_in, n_in/2.0)
    n=n_in
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_value(h,n)
    # anti candidate
    ha=anti_palindromic_h(h,n)
    ca=c5_value(ha,n)
    if ca < best_c: best_c=ca; best_h=ha.copy()
    # Ensure n even? For local/binary, if n odd, best_n maybe odd; pair search supports but binary no. Candidate high even. Maybe if n odd, make even? The parent n likely 250. We can if n%2 and n>3: h = anti? But sum target .5; local okay. We'll keep.
    # parent anti refine
    if n <= 1200 and (deadline - time.time()) > 30:
        end = min(t0 + 0.14*B, deadline - 20)
        if end > time.time()+10:
            phases=make_anti_phases(n)
            ha=anti_refine(best_h,n,phases,end-time.time(),end)
            ca=c5_value(ha,n)
            if ca < best_c - 1e-15: update
            # local on that anti for short? We'll do parent local next.
    # parent local
    end = min(t0 + 0.28*B, deadline-20)
    if end > time.time()+15:
        Kloc = 32 if n <= 1000 else 48
        aloc = max(20.0, 50.0 * max(1.0, n/250.0))
        hloc,cl=local_search_gradient_pairs(best_h,n,end,K=Kloc,alpha=aloc,seed=seed)
        if cl < best_c: update
    # high candidates
    cands=[]
    for mult in (1.5,2.0,3.0,4.0):
       nv=int(round(best_n*mult)) if best_n>0 else 500
       # ensure at least best_n+50? 
       nv=max(nv, best_n+50)
       if nv%2: nv+=1
       if 200 <= nv <= 2048:
          if nv not in cands: cands.append(nv)
    cands.sort()
    # cap number by budget
    max_high = 3 if B > 700 else 2 if B > 350 else 1
    # We may also include if best_n small. Filter <=best_n? We'll filter in loop.
    cands=[nv for nv in cands if nv <= 2048][:max_high]
    # High phase from 0.28B to 0.80B
    if cands and (deadline - time.time()) > 40:
       frac_start=0.28; frac_end=min(0.80, (deadline - t0)/B - 0.15?) # ensure final
       # if dl earlier? Use simple: high_end = max(time.time()+30, min(t0+0.80*B, deadline - max(20,0.20*B)))
       high_end = max(time.time()+30, min(t0 + 0.80*B, deadline - max(20.0, 0.20*B)))
       if high_end > time.time()+20:
          n_high=len(cands)
          seg = (high_end - time.time()) / float(n_high)
          for idx,nv in enumerate(cands):
              if nv <= best_n:
                  continue
              if time.time() >= deadline - 20: break
              ph_end = min(time.time() + seg, high_end, deadline - 20)
              if ph_end < time.time() + 15: continue
              # resample
              hr = resample_h(best_h,best_n,nv)
              hr = anti_palindromic_h(hr,nv)
              cr = c5_value(hr,nv)
              work_h=hr.copy()
              if cr < best_c: update
              # If binary better, choose work
              hbin=binarize_h(hr,nv) if nv%2==0 else hr
              cbin=c5_value(hbin,nv)
              if cbin < cr: work_h=hbin
              if cbin < best_c: update
              # anti refine within phase
              anti_frac=0.60
              anti_end = time.time() + anti_frac*(ph_end-time.time())
              if nv <= 2048 and anti_end > time.time()+10:
                  phases=make_anti_phases(nv)
                  # if nv large, use fewer phases? make 4; okay but budget limited.
                  ha = anti_refine(work_h if cwork? , nv, phases, anti_end-time.time(), anti_end)
                  ca=c5_value(ha,nv)
                  work_h=ha.copy()
                  if ca < best_c: update
                  # binary from anti
                  if nv%2==0:
                      hhb=binarize_h(ha,nv); chb=c5_value(hhb,nv)
                      if chb < c5_value(work_h,nv): work_h=hhb
                      if chb < best_c: update
              # local polish
              local_end = ph_end
              if local_end > time.time()+10:
                  Kloc = 48 if nv > 800 else 32
                  aloc = max(30.0, 80.0 * max(1.0, nv/250.0))
                  hloc,cl = local_search_gradient_pairs(work_h,nv,local_end,K=Kloc,alpha=aloc,seed=seed+100+idx)
                  if cl < best_c: best_c=cl; best_h=hloc.copy(); best_n=nv
    # final polish
    fin_end=deadline-2
    if fin_end > time.time()+10:
        Kloc=48 if best_n>800 else 32
        aloc=max(40.0, 100.0 * max(1.0, best_n/250.0))
        hloc,cl=local_search_gradient_pairs(best_h,best_n,fin_end,K=Kloc,alpha=aloc,seed=seed+777)
        if cl < best_c: update
    # final anti candidate maybe before return
    ha=anti_palindromic_h(best_h,best_n); ca=c5_value(ha,best_n)
    if ca < best_c: update
    # Maybe binary if even
    if best_n%2==0:
       hb=binarize_h(best_h,best_n); cb=c5_value(hb,best_n)
       if cb<best_c: update
    best_h=project_box_mean(best_h,best_n/2.0)
    best_c=c5_value(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def project_box_mean(h,target):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) > 1e-12:
       lo=-1; hi=1; mid=0
       for 80: ...
       h=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]+=min(diff,1-h[idx])
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]+=max(diff,-h[idx]) # diff negative
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global state for L-BFGS-B in the anti-palindromic parameterization.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
_ASTATE = {}
_ACACHE = {'u': None, 'h': None, 'c': None, 'n': -1}


def project_box_mean(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = h.sum()
    if abs(s - target) > 1e-12:
        lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
        mid = 0.0
        for _ in range(80):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            sh = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if sh > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)

    d = target - h.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-15:
        if d > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(d, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            d2 = max(d, -h[idx])
            h[idx] += d2
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def c5_value(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    if n_new == n_old:
        return project_box_mean(h_old, 0.5 * n_new)
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    hh = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_box_mean(hh, 0.5 * n_new)


def binarize_h(h, n):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_box_mean(h, 0.5 * n)
    target = n // 2
    h2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if 0 < target < n:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[-target:]
        h2[idx] = 1.0
    elif target == n:
        h2[:] = 1.0
    return h2


def anti_palindromic_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    rev = h[::-1]
    h2 = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        p = n // 2
        first = 0.5 * (h[:p] + (1.0 - rev[:p]))
        first = np.clip(first, 0.0, 1.0)
        h2[:p] = first
        h2[p:] = 1.0 - first[::-1]
    else:
        p = (n - 1) // 2
        if p > 0:
            first = 0.5 * (h[:p] + (1.0 - rev[:p]))
            first = np.clip(first, 0.0, 1.0)
            h2[:p] = first
            h2[p + 1:] = 1.0 - first[::-1]
        h2[p] = 0.5
    return np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)


def anti_h_from_u(u, n):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        p = n // 2
    else:
        p = (n - 1) // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    if p > 0:
        u_use = np.clip(u[:p], 0.0, 1.0)
        h[:p] = u_use
    else:
        u_use = np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h[p:] = 1.0 - u_use[::-1]
    else:
        h[p] = 0.5
        if p > 0:
            h[p + 1:] = 1.0 - u_use[::-1]
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def smooth_top_value(c, alpha, K):
    sz = int(c.size)
    k = max(1, min(int(K), sz))
    if k == sz:
        vals = c
    else:
        vals = c[np.argpartition(c, sz - k)[-k:]]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        return m
    base = float(alpha) * (vals - m)
    e = np.exp(base)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + np.log(s) / float(alpha)


def soft_weights(c, alpha, K):
    sz = int(c.size)
    k = max(1, min(int(K), sz))
    if k == sz:
        idx = np.arange(sz, dtype=int)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, sz - k)[-k:]
    vals = c[idx]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        return idx, np.full(k, 1.0 / k, dtype=float)
    base = float(alpha) * (vals - m)
    e = np.exp(base)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(k, dtype=float)
        tie = np.flatnonzero(vals >= m - 1e-12)
        if tie.size == 0:
            tie = np.flatnonzero(vals == vals.max())
        w[tie] = 1.0 / tie.size
    else:
        w = e / s
    return idx, w


def grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w):
    n = len(h)
    center = n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for p, wt in zip(idx, w):
        if wt <= 1e-15:
            continue
        s = int(p) - center
        if s > 0:
            m = n - s
            g[s:n] += wt * (1.0 - h[:m])
            g[:m] -= wt * h[s:n]
        elif s < 0:
            t = -s
            m = n - t
            g[:m] += wt * (1.0 - h[t:n])
            g[t:n] -= wt * h[:m]
        else:
            g += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    return g


def gradient_high_low(h, corr, alpha, K):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        empty = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float), empty, empty
    idx, w = soft_weights(corr, alpha, K)
    g = grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w)
    q = max(6, min(n, n // 10 + 6))
    order = np.argsort(g)
    lo = order[:q].copy()
    hi = order[-q:].copy()
    return g, hi, lo


def _anti_curves(u):
    n = int(_ASTATE.get('n', 0))
    p = int(_ASTATE.get('p', 0))
    if u.size != p:
        u2 = np.zeros(max(0, p), dtype=float)
        m = min(p, u.size)
        if m > 0:
            u2[:m] = u[:m]
        u = np.clip(u2, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)

    if _ACACHE['n'] == n and _ACACHE['u'] is not None and len(_ACACHE['u']) == u.size:
        if np.array_equal(_ACACHE['u'], u):
            return _ACACHE['h'], _ACACHE['c']

    h = anti_h_from_u(u, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    _ACACHE['n'] = n
    _ACACHE['u'] = u.copy()
    _ACACHE['h'] = h
    _ACACHE['c'] = c
    return h, c


def _anti_obj(u):
    _, c = _anti_curves(u)
    return float(smooth_top_value(c, _ASTATE['alpha'], _ASTATE['K']))


def _anti_jac(u):
    h, c = _anti_curves(u)
    p = int(_ASTATE['p'])
    if p == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    idx, w = soft_weights(c, _ASTATE['alpha'], _ASTATE['K'])
    gh = grad_from_idx_weights(h, idx, w)
    mirror = _ASTATE['mirror']
    return gh[:p] - gh[mirror]


def make_anti_phases(n):
    scale = max(1.0, float(n) / 250.0)
    K0 = min(2 * n - 1, max(64, n // 2))
    K0 = min(K0, 512)
    Ks = (K0, max(64, K0 // 2), max(32, K0 // 4), max(16, K0 // 8))
    As = (2.0 * scale, 20.0 * scale, 100.0 * scale, 500.0 * scale)
    return list(zip(As, Ks))


def anti_phase(u, n, alpha, K, phase_budget):
    if n % 2 == 0:
        p = n // 2
    else:
        p = (n - 1) // 2
    if p <= 0:
        return u

    _ASTATE['n'] = int(n)
    _ASTATE['p'] = int(p)
    _ASTATE['alpha'] = float(alpha)
    _ASTATE['K'] = int(max(1, K))
    _ASTATE['mirror'] = np.array([n - 1 - i for i in range(p)], dtype=int)
    _ACACHE.clear()

    u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    bounds = ((0.0, 1.0),) * p

    maxiter = 30
    if phase_budget > 6.0:
        t_est = time.time()
        try:
            res2 = minimize(
                _anti_obj, u, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=_anti_jac,
                options={'maxiter': 3, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-10, 'maxls': 20, 'disp': True}
            )
            if res2.x is not None:
                u = np.clip(res2.x, 0.0, 1.0)
            elapsed = max(1e-6, time.time() - t_est)
        except Exception:
            elapsed = max(1e-3, time.time() - t_est)
        per = max(1e-5, elapsed / 3.0)
        if p <= 250:
            cap = 600
        elif p <= 600:
            cap = 350
        else:
            cap = 160
        maxiter = int(max(12, min(cap, 0.70 * phase_budget / per)))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _anti_obj, u, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=_anti_jac,
            options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'maxls': 20, 'disp': False}
        )
        if res.x is not None:
            u = np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.clip(u, 0.0, 1.0)


def anti_refine(h, n, phases, total_budget, deadline):
    h = anti_palindromic_h(h, n)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        p = n // 2
    else:
        p = (n - 1) // 2
    if p <= 0 or not phases:
        return h

    u = h[:p].copy()
    L = len(phases)
    for i, (alpha, K) in enumerate(phases):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        left = max(1, L - i)
        pb = min(float(total_budget) / float(max(1, L)),
                 0.85 * (deadline - time.time()) / float(left))
        if pb <= 4.0:
            break
        u = anti_phase(u, n, alpha, K, pb)
        h = anti_h_from_u(u, n)
    return h


def local_search_gradient_pairs(h0, n, deadline, K=32, alpha=50.0,
                                recompute_interval=40, max_restarts=28, seed=0):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_box_mean(h0, target)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(corr.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cur = cur

    om = 1.0 - h
    om_rev = om[::-1]
    tmp = np.zeros(corr.size, dtype=float)
    buf = np.zeros(corr.size, dtype=float)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    _, hi, lo = gradient_high_low(h, corr, alpha, K)
    attempt = 0
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    accepts = 0

    while attempt < 1000000 and time.time() < deadline:
        if attempt % recompute_interval == 0:
            cur = float(corr.max())
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
                best_cur = cur
                best_h = h.copy()

            if accepts > 0 and accepts % 512 == 0:
                corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = float(corr.max())

            if stall > 520 and restarts < max_restarts and (deadline - time.time()) > 25.0:
                h = project_box_mean(h + 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n), target)
                om = 1.0 - h
                om_rev = om[::-1]
                corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = float(corr.max())
                if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
                    best_cur = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                restarts += 1
                stall = 0

            _, hi, lo = gradient_high_low(h, corr, alpha, K)
            if hi.size == 0 or lo.size == 0:
                break

        if hi.size == 0 or lo.size == 0:
            break

        improved = False
        if rng.rand() < 0.85:
            src = int(hi[rng.randint(hi.size)])
        else:
            src = 0
            for j in range(n):
                if 1e-12 < h[j] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                    src = j
                    break

        for _ in range(8):
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            if rng.rand() < 0.85:
                dst = int(lo[rng.randint(lo.size)])
            else:
                dst = 0
                for j in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
                    if 1e-12 < 1.0 - h[j] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                        dst = j
                        break
            if src == dst:
                continue

            avail = min(h[src], 1.0 - h[dst])
            if avail <= 1e-11:
                continue

            for ratio in (1.0, 0.5):
                t = ratio * avail
                if t <= 1e-13:
                    continue

                tmp.fill(0.0)

                # Add +t at dst
                tmp[dst:dst + n] += t * om_rev
                tmp[n - 1 - dst:2 * n - 1 - dst] -= t * h

                # Add -t at src
                tmp[src:src + n] -= t * om_rev
                tmp[n - 1 - src:2 * n - 1 - src] += t * h

                zz = t * t
                tmp[n - 1] -= 2.0 * zz
                if src != dst:
                    tmp[n - 1 + (dst - src)] += zz
                    tmp[n - 1 + (src - dst)] += zz

                np.add(corr, tmp, out=buf)
                nm = float(buf.max())
                if nm < cur - 1e-12:
                    h[src] -= t
                    h[dst] += t
                    if h[src] < 0.0:
                        h[src] = 0.0
                    if h[dst] > 1.0:
                        h[dst] = 1.0
                    om[src] = 1.0 - h[src]
                    om[dst] = 1.0 - h[dst]

                    np.add(corr, tmp, out=corr)
                    cur = nm
                    best_cur = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    accepts += 1
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        attempt += 1

    best_h = project_box_mean(best_h, target)
    cc = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode='full')
    return best_h, float(np.max(cc) * (2.0 / n))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    B = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + B - 4.0

    try:
        h_in = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h_in = np.zeros(250, dtype=float)
        h_in[0::2] = 1.0

    n_in = len(h_in)
    if n_in < 2:
        h_in = np.array([0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0], dtype=float)
        n_in = len(h_in)

    h = project_box_mean(h_in, 0.5 * n_in)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_n = n_in
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, best_n)

    ha = anti_palindromic_h(best_h, best_n)
    ca = c5_value(ha, best_n)
    if ca < best_c:
        best_h = ha.copy()
        best_c = ca

    # Parent-resolution anti constrained architecture search.
    if best_n <= 1200 and (deadline - t0) > 35.0:
        end = min(t0 + 0.14 * B, deadline - 18.0)
        if end > time.time() + 12.0:
            h_anti = anti_refine(best_h, best_n, make_anti_phases(best_n),
                                 end - time.time(), end)
            ca = c5_value(h_anti, best_n)
            if ca < best_c:
                best_h = h_anti.copy()
                best_c = ca

    # A first full-space polish at the parent resolution.
    end = min(t0 + 0.28 * B, deadline - 18.0)
    if end > time.time() + 15.0:
        Kloc = 32 if best_n <= 1000 else 48
        aloc = max(25.0, 60.0 * max(1.0, float(best_n) / 250.0))
        hloc, cloc = local_search_gradient_pairs(
            best_h, best_n, end, K=Kloc, alpha=aloc, seed=seed
        )
        if cloc < best_c:
            best_h = hloc.copy()
            best_c = cloc
            best_n = int(best_n)

    # Higher-resolution candidates.  Keep the largest practical size for
    # O(n^2) correlation evaluations.
    max_opt_n = 1200
    cands = []
    for mult in (2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
        nv = int(round(max(1, n_in) * mult))
        if nv % 2:
            nv += 1
        nv = max(nv, 160)
        if nv <= max_opt_n and nv not in cands:
            cands.append(nv)
    cands.sort()
    if B <= 900:
        if len(cands) >= 2:
            cands = [cands[0], cands[-1]]
    max_high_attempts = 3 if B > 900 else (2 if B > 420 else 1)

    if B > 180.0 and cands:
        high_end = min(t0 + 0.80 * B, deadline - max(20.0, 0.20 * B))
        if high_end > time.time() + 30.0:
            for hi_idx in range(max_high_attempts):
                if time.time() >= deadline - 25.0:
                    break

                if hi_idx < len(cands):
                    nv = cands[hi_idx]
                else:
                    nv = int(round(1.6 * max(1, best_n)))
                if nv <= best_n:
                    nv = int(round(max(best_n + 120, 1.6 * best_n)))
                if nv % 2:
                    nv += 1

                if nv <= best_n or nv > max_opt_n:
                    break

                phase_end = min(time.time() + (high_end - time.time()) / float(max(1, max_high_attempts - hi_idx)),
                                high_end, deadline - 25.0)
                if phase_end < time.time() + 20.0:
                    continue

                h_res = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nv)
                c_res = c5_value(h_res, nv)
                work_h = h_res.copy()
                work_c = c_res

                h_ant = anti_palindromic_h(h_res, nv)
                c_ant = c5_value(h_ant, nv)
                if c_ant < work_c:
                    work_h = h_ant.copy()
                    work_c = c_ant

                if work_c < best_c:
                    best_h = work_h.copy()
                    best_c = work_c
                    best_n = nv

                h_bin = binarize_h(h_res, nv)
                c_bin = c5_value(h_bin, nv)
                if c_bin < best_c:
                    best_h = h_bin.copy()
                    best_c = c_bin
                    best_n = nv
                if c_bin < work_c:
                    work_h = h_bin.copy()
                    work_c = c_bin

                anti_end = time.time() + 0.58 * (phase_end - time.time())
                if anti_end > time.time() + 12.0:
                    h_ant2 = anti_refine(h_ant, nv, make_anti_phases(nv),
                                         anti_end - time.time(), anti_end)
                    c_ant2 = c5_value(h_ant2, nv)
                    if c_ant2 < best_c:
                        best_h = h_ant2.copy()
                        best_c = c_ant2
                        best_n = nv
                    if c_ant2 < work_c:
                        work_h = h_ant2.copy()
                        work_c = c_ant2

                    h_bin2 = binarize_h(h_ant2, nv)
                    c_bin2 = c5_value(h_bin2, nv)
                    if c_bin2 < best_c:
                        best_h = h_bin2.copy()
                        best_c = c_bin2
                        best_n = nv
                    if c_bin2 < work_c:
                        work_h = h_bin2.copy()
                        work_c = c_bin2

                local_end = phase_end
                if local_end > time.time() + 15.0:
                    Kloc = 48 if nv > 800 else 32
                    aloc = max(35.0, 100.0 * max(1.0, float(nv) / 250.0))
                    hloc, cloc = local_search_gradient_pairs(
                        work_h, nv, local_end, K=Kloc, alpha=aloc,
                        seed=seed + 100 + hi_idx
                    )
                    if cloc < best_c:
                        best_h = hloc.copy()
                        best_c = cloc
                        best_n = nv

    # Final polish at the best resolution.
    final_end = deadline - 2.0
    if final_end > time.time() + 12.0:
        Kloc = 48 if best_n > 800 else 32
        aloc = max(45.0, 120.0 * max(1.0, float(best_n) / 250.0))
        hloc, cloc = local_search_gradient_pairs(
            best_h, best_n, final_end, K=Kloc, alpha=aloc, seed=seed + 777
        )
        if cloc < best_c:
            best_h = hloc.copy()
            best_c = cloc
            best_n = int(best_n)

    # Last cheap symmetry / binary candidates.
    ha = anti_palindromic_h(best_h, best_n)
    ca = c5_value(ha, best_n)
    if ca < best_c:
        best_h = ha.copy()
        best_c = ca

    if best_n % 2 == 0:
        hb = binarize_h(best_h, best_n)
        cb = c5_value(hb, best_n)
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h = hb.copy()
            best_c = cb

    best_h = project_box_mean(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
